<DESCRIPTION>EXHIBIT 1.01
<TEXT>
<html>
  <head>
    <title></title>
    <!-- Licensed to: Broadridge Financial Solutions, Inc.
         Document created using Broadridge PROfile 25.3.1.5296
         Copyright 1995 - 2025 Broadridge -->
  </head>
<body bgcolor="#ffffff" style="margin: auto; width: 8.5in; color: #000000; font-family: Arial; font-size: 10pt; text-align: left;">
  <div style="width: 100%;" class="BRPFPageHeader"></div>
  <div>
    <hr noshade="noshade" align="center" style="height: 4px; color: #000000; background-color: #000000; text-align: center; margin-left: auto; margin-right: auto; border: none;">
    <div>
      <div>
        <div style="text-align: right; color: rgb(0, 0, 0); font-size: 14pt;"><font style="font-size: 12pt; font-weight: bold;"> Exhibit 1.01</font><br>
        </div>
        <div style="text-align: center; color: rgb(0, 0, 0); font-size: 16pt; font-weight: bold;">Conflict Minerals Report 2024</div>
      </div>
      <div>
        <div><br>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div style="font-size: 11pt;">
        <div style="color: rgb(0, 0, 0); font-weight: bold; font-size: 14pt;">Overview of Apple&#8217;s commitment to responsible sourcing</div>
      </div>
      <div>
        <div style="font-size: 11pt;"><br>
        </div>
        <div style="text-align: justify; color: rgb(0, 0, 0); font-size: 11pt;">At Apple, our respect for human rights begins with our commitment to treating everyone with dignity and respect. In alignment with the United Nations Guiding Principles on
          Business and Human Rights (&#8220;UN Guiding Principles&#8221;), we conduct human rights and environmental due diligence in our supply chain to identify risks and work to mitigate them. We also work to identify and remedy potential adverse impacts, track and
          measure our progress, and report our findings.</div>
        <div style="font-size: 11pt;"><br>
        </div>
        <div style="text-align: justify; color: rgb(0, 0, 0); font-size: 11pt;">Apple is committed to sourcing, using, and recycling the materials that we rely on in a way that meets our high standards for labor, human rights, and environmental
          stewardship. We aim to one day make our products and packaging solely from responsibly sourced recycled or renewable materials &#8212; while maintaining our same rigorous standards of quality and durability for our products. By the end of 2025, Apple
          plans to use 100 percent recycled cobalt in all Apple-designed batteries<sup style="vertical-align: text-top; line-height: 1; font-size: smaller;">1</sup>; 100 percent recycled tin soldering and 100 percent recycled gold plating in all Apple-designed rigid and flexible printed circuit boards; and 100 percent
          recycled rare earth elements in all magnets across all products.</div>
        <div style="font-size: 11pt;"><br>
        </div>
        <div style="text-align: justify; color: rgb(0, 0, 0); font-size: 11pt;">As we make progress toward these goals, we continue our work to source tin, tungsten, tantalum and gold (&#8220;3TG&#8221;) and other minerals responsibly. Although Apple does not source
          primary material directly from mine sites, our responsible minerals sourcing program includes requirements that apply to all levels of Apple&#8217;s supply chain.</div>
        <div style="font-size: 11pt;"><br>
        </div>
        <div style="text-align: justify; color: rgb(0, 0, 0); font-size: 11pt;">As of December 31, 2024 &#8212; for the tenth consecutive year &#8212; 100 percent of the identified smelters and refiners in our supply chain for all applicable Apple products
          manufactured during 2024 participated in an independent third-party conflict minerals audit for 3TG. These audits encompassed the identified smelters and refiners that provide materials for the following Apple product categories: iPhone<sup style="vertical-align: text-top; line-height: 1; font-size: smaller;">&#174;</sup>,
          Mac<sup style="vertical-align: text-top; line-height: 1; font-size: smaller;">&#174;</sup>, iPad<sup style="vertical-align: text-top; line-height: 1; font-size: smaller;">&#174;</sup>, AirPods<sup style="vertical-align: text-top; line-height: 1; font-size: smaller;">&#174;</sup>, Apple TV<sup style="vertical-align: text-top; line-height: 1; font-size: smaller;">&#174;</sup>, Apple Watch<sup style="vertical-align: text-top; line-height: 1; font-size: smaller;">&#174;</sup>, Apple Vision Pro<sup style="vertical-align: text-top; line-height: 1; font-size: smaller;">TM</sup>, Beats<sup style="vertical-align: text-top; line-height: 1; font-size: smaller;">&#174;</sup> products, HomePod<sup style="vertical-align: text-top; line-height: 1; font-size: smaller;">&#174;</sup>, HomePod mini<sup style="vertical-align: text-top; line-height: 1; font-size: smaller;">&#174;</sup>, Apple Card<sup style="vertical-align: text-top; line-height: 1; font-size: smaller;">&#174;</sup>, and all Apple<sup style="vertical-align: text-top; line-height: 1; font-size: smaller;">&#174;</sup>
          accessories.</div>
        <div style="font-size: 11pt;"><br>
        </div>
        <div style="text-align: justify; color: rgb(0, 0, 0); font-size: 11pt;">Based on our due diligence, including analyzing the information provided by third-party audit programs, upstream traceability programs, independent reporting, and our
          suppliers, we found no reasonable basis for concluding that any smelters or refiners of 3TG identified in our supply chain as of December 31, 2024 directly or indirectly financed or benefited armed groups in the Democratic Republic of the Congo
          (&#8220;DRC&#8221;) or an adjoining country.</div>
        <div style="font-size: 11pt;"><br>
        </div>
        <div style="text-align: justify; color: rgb(0, 0, 0); font-size: 11pt;">In response to reports of escalating regional conflict, as well as smuggling and illegal taxation, in June 2024 Apple issued a notification to its suppliers to cease the
          sourcing, directly or indirectly, of 3TG for Apple parts and products from the DRC and Rwanda.</div>
        <div style="font-size: 11pt;"><br>
        </div>
        <div style="text-align: justify; color: rgb(0, 0, 0); font-size: 11pt;">Apple remains committed to supporting local organizations that do vital work in and around mining communities. In addition to our work to strengthen industrywide due diligence
          programs operating in areas where 3TG minerals are sourced, we engage with and support a broad range of multistakeholder and community initiatives. Input from stakeholders and rights-holders across our supply chain contributes to our robust due
          diligence program and we believe drives industrywide progress.</div>
        <div style="text-align: justify; color: rgb(0, 0, 0); font-size: 11pt;"> <br>
          <hr noshade="noshade" align="left" style="background-color: #000000; border-bottom: 0px none; border-left: 0px none; border-right: 0px none; border-top: 0px none; margin: 0px auto 0px 0px; height: 1px; width: 25%; color: #000000;">
          <div>
            <div style="color: rgb(0, 0, 0); font-size: 10pt;"><sup style="vertical-align: text-top; line-height: 1; font-size: smaller;">1</sup> Apple&#8217;s commitment is to use 100 percent recycled cobalt, using mass balance allocation, in all Apple-designed batteries by 2025.</div>
            <div style="color: #000000;">&#160;</div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div style="clear: both; margin-top: 10pt; margin-bottom: 10pt;" class="BRPFPageBreakArea">
        <div style="width: 100%;" class="BRPFPageFooter"><font style="font-size: 8pt;"></font>
          <div style="color: rgb(0, 0, 0);">
            <div>
              <div style="text-align: center; color: rgb(0, 0, 0); font-weight: normal; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-transform: none;">Apple Inc. | 2024 Conflict Minerals Report |&#160; <font class="BRPFPageNumber">1</font></div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div style="page-break-after: always;" class="BRPFPageBreak">
          <hr noshade="noshade" style="margin: 4px 0px; width: 100%; border-width: 0; height: 2px; color: #000000; background-color: #000000; clear: both;"></div>
        <div style="width: 100%;" class="BRPFPageHeader"></div>
      </div>
      <div>
        <div style="text-align: justify; color: rgb(0, 0, 0); font-size: 11pt;">The below chart summarizes the comprehensive set of tools we utilize to drive progress throughout our supply chain.<sup style="vertical-align: text-top; line-height: 1; font-size: smaller;">2</sup></div>
        <div style="text-align: justify;"><br>
        </div>
        <div style="text-align: center;"><img width="600" border="0" height="298" src="image00001.jpg"></div>
        <div><br>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div style="font-size: 14pt;">
        <div style="color: rgb(0, 0, 0); font-weight: bold;">OECD Step 1: Establish strong company management systems</div>
      </div>
      <div>
        <div><br>
        </div>
        <div style="text-align: justify; color: rgb(0, 0, 0); font-size: 11pt;">Apple has robust internal policies and management systems overseeing its efforts for the responsible sourcing of minerals. We are committed to meeting and exceeding the
          expectations for responsible sourcing of primary minerals and recycled materials outlined in the Organisation for Economic Co-operation and Development&#8217;s (&#8220;OECD&#8221;) 2016 Due Diligence Guidance for Responsible Supply Chains of Minerals from
          Conflict-Affected and High-Risk Areas and related Supplements (&#8220;OECD Due Diligence Guidance&#8221;). Conducting human rights and environmental due diligence in alignment with the OECD Due Diligence Guidance and the UN Guiding Principles is the
          foundation of Apple&#8217;s responsible sourcing program for primary and recycled materials.</div>
        <div style="font-size: 11pt;"><br>
        </div>
        <div style="text-align: justify; color: rgb(0, 0, 0); font-size: 11pt;">Apple seeks to conduct business ethically, honestly, and in compliance with applicable laws and regulations. This applies to every business decision in every area of the
          company worldwide.</div>
        <div><br>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div style="font-size: 11pt;">
        <div style="color: rgb(0, 0, 0); font-weight: bold;">Apple&#8217;s Supplier Code of Conduct and Responsible Sourcing of Materials Standard</div>
      </div>
      <div>
        <div><br>
        </div>
        <div style="text-align: justify; color: rgb(0, 0, 0); font-size: 11pt;">Apple&#8217;s Supplier Code of Conduct (the &#8220;Code&#8221;), which includes Apple&#8217;s Supplier Responsibility Standard on the Responsible Sourcing of Materials (&#8220;Responsible Sourcing
          Standard&#8221;) requires suppliers, smelters, refiners, and recyclers in our supply chain to identify and assess a broad range of risks beyond conflict, including social, environmental, and human rights risks. It applies to all levels of Apple&#8217;s
          supply chain, including traders, suppliers, sub-suppliers, mining companies, and operators of collection points for recycled materials used in Apple products. Suppliers are also required to review reported incidents and public allegations linked
          to their smelters and refiners, and to participate in 3TG traceability and independent third-party audit programs to mitigate identified risks.</div>
      </div>
      <br>
      <hr noshade="noshade" align="left" style="background-color: #000000; border-bottom: 0px none; border-left: 0px none; border-right: 0px none; border-top: 0px none; margin: 0px auto 0px 0px; height: 1px; width: 25%; color: #000000;">
      <div>
        <div style="color: #000000;"><sup style="vertical-align: text-top; line-height: 1; font-size: smaller;">2</sup> More information on Apple&#8217;s Responsible Minerals Sourcing program is available at www.apple.com/supply-chain/ </div>
        <div style="color: #000000;"> <br>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div style="clear: both; margin-top: 10pt; margin-bottom: 10pt;" class="BRPFPageBreakArea">
        <div style="width: 100%;" class="BRPFPageFooter"><font style="font-size: 8pt;"></font>
          <div style="color: rgb(0, 0, 0);">
            <div>
              <div style="text-align: center; color: rgb(0, 0, 0); font-weight: normal; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-transform: none;">Apple Inc. | 2024 Conflict Minerals Report |&#160; <font class="BRPFPageNumber">2</font></div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div style="page-break-after: always;" class="BRPFPageBreak">
          <hr noshade="noshade" style="margin: 4px 0px; width: 100%; border-width: 0; height: 2px; color: #000000; background-color: #000000; clear: both;"></div>
        <div style="width: 100%;" class="BRPFPageHeader"></div>
      </div>
      <div>
        <div style="text-align: justify; color: rgb(0, 0, 0); font-size: 11pt;">The Code and Responsible Sourcing Standard are based on industry best practices and internationally accepted principles, including the UN Guiding Principles, the International
          Labour Organization&#8217;s International Labour Standards, and the OECD Due Diligence Guidance.</div>
        <div style="font-size: 11pt;"><br>
        </div>
        <div style="text-align: justify; color: rgb(0, 0, 0); font-size: 11pt;">The Code outlines Apple&#8217;s requirements for its suppliers in the areas of labor and human rights, health and safety, the environment, ethics, and management systems. The
          Responsible Sourcing Standard specifically outlines Apple&#8217;s extensive requirements on the responsible sourcing of minerals and other materials, including expectations for suppliers concerning 3TG due diligence and related sourcing matters. The
          Code, including the Responsible Sourcing Standard, is available in 18 languages and evaluated and strengthened regularly, raising the bar suppliers must meet.</div>
        <div style="text-align: justify;"><font style="color: #000000;"> </font><br>
        </div>
      </div>

                <div style="font-size: 11pt;">Environment and Supply Chain Innovation (&#8220;ESCI&#8221;) team: Within Apple&#8217;s Worldwide Operations group, ESCI has primary responsibility for upholding Apple&#8217;s values across its global supply chain. The ESCI team
                  coordinates efforts related to Apple&#8217;s Code and Responsible Sourcing Standard and works across Apple&#8217;s business teams and functions, including product design, manufacturing operations, environmental initiatives, procurement, legal,
                  finance, and retail. The ESCI team also regularly reports to, and consults with, Apple&#8217;s senior management to review progress and set ongoing strategies for our responsible sourcing of materials and human rights and environmental due

      <div>
        <div style="text-align: justify; color: rgb(0, 0, 0); font-size: 11pt; font-weight: bold;">Board oversight</div>
        <div style="font-size: 11pt;"><br>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div>
        <table cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" style="font-family: Arial; font-size: 10pt; width: 100%; text-align: left; color: #000000;" class="DSPFListTable" id="zf6a26cba22ed4234a023f9ab58cff8f1">

        <table cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" style="font-family: Arial; font-size: 10pt; width: 100%; text-align: left; color: #000000;" class="DSPFListTable" id="z3f5d573bcdcb4ba6a2616f71f8003424">

        <table cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" style="font-family: Arial; font-size: 10pt; width: 100%; text-align: left; color: #000000;" class="DSPFListTable" id="z2b6a5a68725840bcbc86515ea5bfa69f">

              <td style="width: auto; vertical-align: top; text-align: justify;">
                <div><font style="font-size: 11pt;">Nominating and Corporate Governance Committee: Consisting entirely of independent directors, the committee assists Apple&#8217;s Board of Directors in overseeing Apple&#8217;s strategies, policies, and practices
                    relating to environmental and social matters.</font></div>
              </td>
            </tr>

        </table>
      </div>
      <div>
        <div><br>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div style="font-size: 11pt;">
        <div style="color: rgb(0, 0, 0); font-weight: bold;">Supplier engagement</div>
      </div>
      <div>
        <div><br>
        </div>
        <div style="text-align: justify; color: rgb(0, 0, 0); font-size: 11pt;">Apple requires its suppliers to adhere to the Code and the Responsible Sourcing Standard, including any subsequent amendments or updates. Each year, through our Code assessment
          process, suppliers are evaluated against more than 500 criteria to verify their compliance and identify areas for improvement. These assessments are conducted globally by independent, third-party auditing firms that are accredited to meet
          international auditing standards. For select suppliers that report to Apple the use of 3TG in their parts and products this may also include working with an independent audit firm to conduct specialized responsible sourcing audits. Following
          audits, suppliers are given a list of areas to strengthen with regard to the Code and Responsible Sourcing Standard, and the supplier is required to correct these areas within a specified timeframe.</div>
        <div style="font-size: 11pt;"><br>
        </div>
        <div style="text-align: justify; color: rgb(0, 0, 0); font-size: 11pt;">Suppliers are also required to apply Apple&#8217;s requirements upstream to their own suppliers throughout all levels of the supply chain. In this way, and through direct outreach by
          Apple to all 3TG smelters and refiners identified in its supply chain, Apple implements its requirement that smelters and refiners in its supply chain comply with Apple&#8217;s strict standards, including participation in independent third-party audit
          programs.</div>
        <div style="font-size: 11pt;"><br>
        </div>
        <div style="text-align: justify; color: rgb(0, 0, 0); font-size: 11pt;">We annually communicate our 3TG sourcing requirements to suppliers. Additionally, throughout the year we engage with suppliers using tailored communication and guidance,
          including:</div>
        <div style="font-size: 11pt;"><br>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div>
        <table cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" style="font-family: Arial; font-size: 10pt; width: 100%; text-align: left; color: #000000;" class="DSPFListTable" id="z3079c198c60343d7a61b437851e4ce73">

            <tr>
              <td style="width: 18pt; font-size: 11pt;"><br>
              </td>
              <td style="width: 18pt; vertical-align: top; color: rgb(0, 0, 0); font-size: 11pt;">&#8226;</td>
              <td style="width: auto; vertical-align: top; text-align: justify; font-size: 11pt;">
                <div>Conducting annual 3TG due diligence training webinars with suppliers that have reported to Apple the use of 3TG in parts and products.</div>
              </td>
            </tr>

        </table>
        <table cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" style="font-family: Arial; font-size: 10pt; width: 100%; text-align: left; color: #000000;" class="DSPFListTable" id="zb455f54f72e647e5bd4f1e9a04d861ee">

            <tr>
              <td style="width: 18pt; font-size: 11pt;"><br>
              </td>
              <td style="width: 18pt; vertical-align: top; color: rgb(0, 0, 0); font-size: 11pt;">&#8226;</td>
              <td style="width: auto; vertical-align: top; text-align: justify; font-size: 11pt;">
                <div>Providing suppliers access to SupplierCare, our supplier communication platform, which enables suppliers to reach out to us with questions at any time.</div>
              </td>
            </tr>

        </table>
        <table cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" style="font-family: Arial; font-size: 10pt; width: 100%; text-align: left; color: #000000;" class="DSPFListTable" id="z666f3a328b9e4e90a7392db8d9d78de8">

            <tr>
              <td style="width: 18pt; font-size: 11pt;"><br>
              </td>
              <td style="width: 18pt; vertical-align: top; color: rgb(0, 0, 0); font-size: 11pt;">&#8226;</td>
              <td style="width: auto; vertical-align: top; text-align: justify; font-size: 11pt;">
                <div>Offering online training materials in multiple languages that focus on Apple&#8217;s due diligence expectations and requirements for 3TG reporting, among other topics.</div>
              </td>
            </tr>

        </table>
        <table cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" style="font-family: Arial; font-size: 10pt; width: 100%; text-align: left; color: #000000;" class="DSPFListTable" id="z611d1baf6e1048c7974759ced850e3b9">

            <tr>
              <td style="width: 18pt; font-size: 11pt;"><br>
              </td>
              <td style="width: 18pt; vertical-align: top; color: rgb(0, 0, 0); font-size: 11pt;">&#8226;</td>
              <td style="width: auto; vertical-align: top; text-align: justify; font-size: 11pt;">
                <div>Maintaining a dedicated Apple email that allows suppliers to report concerns or grievances related to 3TG mining, processing, and trading. The concerns or grievances submitted are reviewed by Apple, and follow-up activities are
                  conducted when appropriate.</div>
              </td>
            </tr>

        </table>
      </div>
      <div>
        <div style="font-size: 11pt;"><br>
        </div>
        <div style="text-align: justify; color: rgb(0, 0, 0); font-size: 11pt;">If we discover our standards are not being met, we provide support to help suppliers complete a corrective action plan, in line with the OECD Due Diligence Guidance framework
          of progressive improvement, to meet and exceed our requirements within the timeline identified as a result of the assessment. Timelines for corrective actions typically range between 30 and 90 days. If a supplier is unwilling or unable to meet
          Apple&#8217;s requirements, they risk removal from our supply chain.</div>
        <div><br>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div style="clear: both; margin-top: 10pt; margin-bottom: 10pt;" class="BRPFPageBreakArea">
        <div style="width: 100%;" class="BRPFPageFooter"><font style="font-size: 8pt;"></font>
          <div style="color: rgb(0, 0, 0);">
            <div>
              <div style="text-align: center; color: rgb(0, 0, 0); font-weight: normal; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-transform: none;">Apple Inc. | 2024 Conflict Minerals Report |&#160; <font class="BRPFPageNumber">4</font></div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div style="page-break-after: always;" class="BRPFPageBreak">
          <hr noshade="noshade" style="margin: 4px 0px; width: 100%; border-width: 0; height: 2px; color: #000000; background-color: #000000; clear: both;"></div>
        <div style="width: 100%;" class="BRPFPageHeader"></div>
      </div>
      <div>
        <div style="text-align: justify; color: rgb(0, 0, 0); font-size: 11pt;">We also utilize information from industrywide grievance and allegations mechanisms at the smelter and refiner level and have supported whistleblowing programs deeper in the
          supply chain at the mining level. These programs are discussed further in Step 2 of this report.</div>
        <div style="font-size: 11pt;"><br>
        </div>
        <div style="text-align: justify; color: rgb(0, 0, 0); font-size: 11pt;">In June 2024, due to escalating regional conflict and taking into consideration reports from external expert stakeholders &#8212; including the United Nations Group of Experts on the
          DRC, the United States Department of State, and the Responsible Minerals Initiative (&#8220;RMI&#8221;), Apple issued a notification to its suppliers to cease the sourcing, directly or indirectly, of 3TG for Apple parts and products from the DRC and Rwanda.
          Apple has conducted ongoing engagement with suppliers to address questions, and to help suppliers as they work to adjust their sourcing in alignment with the notification.<br>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div>
        <div style="font-size: 11pt;"><br>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div style="font-size: 11pt;">
        <div style="color: rgb(0, 0, 0); font-weight: bold;">Civil society and industry engagement</div>
      </div>
      <div>
        <div style="font-size: 11pt;"><br>
        </div>
        <div style="text-align: justify; color: rgb(0, 0, 0); font-size: 11pt;">We are committed to working in collaboration with stakeholders beyond our supply chain. As part of this commitment, we regularly review and gather feedback on Apple&#8217;s programs
          and wider industry initiatives by engaging with experts from civil society, non-governmental organizations (&#8220;NGOs&#8221;), industry, academia, and government on human rights, the environment, and minerals sourcing. We work with these groups, including
          through convening expert roundtables, to discuss strategies for increasing the effectiveness and innovation of approaches to the responsible sourcing of recycled and primary sourced minerals in supply chains.</div>
        <div style="font-size: 11pt;"><br>
        </div>
        <div style="text-align: justify; color: rgb(0, 0, 0); font-size: 11pt;">In 2024, we continued our active participation and leadership in multiple industry associations and multistakeholder initiatives. Apple representatives were featured on
          multiple panels at the Responsible Business Alliance&#8217;s (&#8220;RBA&#8221;) and RMI annual conference and continued to serve on:</div>
        <div style="font-size: 11pt;"><br>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div>
        <table cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" style="font-family: Arial; font-size: 10pt; width: 100%; text-align: left; color: #000000;" class="DSPFListTable" id="zb9e694f158564d7d87b6e50ebe395747">

            <tr>
              <td style="width: 18pt; font-size: 11pt;"><br>
              </td>
              <td style="width: 18pt; vertical-align: top; color: rgb(0, 0, 0); font-size: 11pt;">&#8226;</td>
              <td style="width: auto; vertical-align: top; text-align: justify; font-size: 11pt;">
                <div>The Steering Committee of the RMI</div>
              </td>
            </tr>

        </table>
        <table cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" style="font-family: Arial; font-size: 10pt; width: 100%; text-align: left; color: #000000;" class="DSPFListTable" id="zd120a7054fa043628903f3f7ab737ac8">

            <tr>
              <td style="width: 18pt; font-size: 11pt;"><br>
              </td>
              <td style="width: 18pt; vertical-align: top; color: rgb(0, 0, 0); font-size: 11pt;">&#8226;</td>
              <td style="width: auto; vertical-align: top; text-align: justify; font-size: 11pt;">
                <div>The Governance Committee of the Public Private Alliance for Responsible Minerals Trade (&#8220;PPA&#8221;)</div>
              </td>
            </tr>

        </table>
        <table cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" style="font-family: Arial; font-size: 10pt; width: 100%; text-align: left; color: #000000;" class="DSPFListTable" id="z8b0c73b681234d76a01bd1dd67ad4e53">

            <tr>
              <td style="width: 18pt; font-size: 11pt;"><br>
              </td>
              <td style="width: 18pt; vertical-align: top; color: rgb(0, 0, 0); font-size: 11pt;">&#8226;</td>
              <td style="width: auto; vertical-align: top; text-align: justify; font-size: 11pt;">
                <div>The United States State Department Advisory Committee on Responsible Business Conduct</div>
              </td>
            </tr>

        </table>
        <table cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" style="font-family: Arial; font-size: 10pt; width: 100%; text-align: left; color: #000000;" class="DSPFListTable" id="z42a8d38414314065a0eb84c87842ed73">

            <tr>
              <td style="width: 18pt; font-size: 11pt;"><br>
              </td>
              <td style="width: 18pt; vertical-align: top; color: rgb(0, 0, 0); font-size: 11pt;">&#8226;</td>
              <td style="width: auto; vertical-align: top; text-align: justify; font-size: 11pt;">
                <div>The OECD&#8217;s Multistakeholder Steering Group</div>

      </div>
      <div>
        <div><br>
        </div>
        <div style="clear: both; margin-top: 10pt; margin-bottom: 10pt;" class="BRPFPageBreakArea">
          <div style="width: 100%;" class="BRPFPageFooter"><font style="font-size: 8pt;"></font>
            <div style="color: rgb(0, 0, 0);">
              <div>
                <div style="text-align: center; color: rgb(0, 0, 0); font-weight: normal; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-transform: none;">Apple Inc. | 2024 Conflict Minerals Report |&#160; <font class="BRPFPageNumber">5</font></div>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div style="page-break-after: always;" class="BRPFPageBreak">
            <hr noshade="noshade" style="margin: 4px 0px; width: 100%; border-width: 0; height: 2px; color: #000000; background-color: #000000; clear: both;"></div>
          <div style="width: 100%;" class="BRPFPageHeader"></div>
        </div>
        <div style="color: rgb(0, 0, 0); font-size: 14pt; font-weight: bold;">OECD Step 2: Identify and assess risks in the supply chain</div>
      </div>
      <div>
        <div><br>
        </div>
        <div style="text-align: justify; color: rgb(0, 0, 0); font-size: 11pt;">We work at multiple levels in our supply chain to continuously identify and assess risks in our minerals supply chains. The priority materials outlined in our Material Impact
          Profiles<sup style="vertical-align: text-top; line-height: 1; font-size: smaller;">3</sup> were identified based, in part, on their salient environmental and human rights supply chain risks. We use this analysis to inform our process to map and conduct enhanced due diligence on priority materials in Apple&#8217;s supply
          chain, including high-risk materials, such as gold. We also work with industry to promote effective and transparent systems that allow these and other materials to be sourced responsibly by others.</div>
        <div style="font-size: 11pt;"><br>
        </div>
        <div style="text-align: justify; color: rgb(0, 0, 0); font-size: 11pt;">A foundational step in Apple&#8217;s 3TG risk assessment process is the requirement that all suppliers that utilize 3TG for Apple parts and/or products submit to Apple an
          industry-standard Conflict Minerals Reporting Template (&#8220;CMRT&#8221;). We collect and process data provided by suppliers through their completion of the CMRT to map our supply chain to the smelter and refiner level and, to the extent available, to the
          mining level. Our Responsible Sourcing Standard requires suppliers to identify risks in their minerals supply chains, including risks outlined in Annex II of the OECD Due Diligence Guidance, such as conflict or human rights risks associated with
          3TG.</div>
        <div style="font-size: 11pt;"><br>
        </div>
        <div style="text-align: justify; color: rgb(0, 0, 0); font-size: 11pt;">We continue to innovate, develop, and scale tools to enhance risk identification and assessment beginning at the mining level, and emphasize the importance of cross-industry
          and multistakeholder collaboration in the use and refinement of these tools in order to drive greater collective impact across global supply chains. This includes a variety of tools aimed at enhancing risk identification and assessment for RMI
          members, such as the Risk Readiness Assessment, Grievance Mechanism, and Materials Insights Platform tools. In 2024, we also continued our work with the natural resource-focused NGO, IMPACT, to support the continued development of solutions to
          measure and track supply chain activities related to social and environmental well-being in artisanal and small-scale mining (&#8220;ASM&#8221;) communities.</div>
        <div style="font-size: 11pt;"><br>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div style="font-size: 11pt;">
        <div style="color: rgb(0, 0, 0); font-weight: bold;">Supporting mining communities</div>
      </div>
      <div>
        <div style="font-size: 11pt;"><br>
        </div>
        <div style="text-align: justify; color: rgb(0, 0, 0); font-size: 11pt;">Apple believes in supporting systemic progress in mineral supply chain due diligence, which requires us to work to address allegations beyond those that could affect Apple&#8217;s
          own supply chain. Accordingly, we have taken steps to engage with a broad group of stakeholders to identify, assess, and mitigate risks collectively. This includes reviewing public allegations from civil society groups and analyzing investigative
          reports by international organizations, including NGOs and the United Nations Group of Experts on the DRC.</div>
        <div style="font-size: 11pt;"><br>
        </div>
        <div style="text-align: justify; color: rgb(0, 0, 0); font-size: 11pt;">Apple believes that supporting rights-holders at the mining level is critical to identifying and assessing risks in 3TG supply chains. In 2024, we continued our support for
          human rights and environmental defenders, rights awareness and vocational education programs, and whistleblowing mechanisms in mining areas in the DRC.</div>
        <div style="font-size: 11pt;"><br>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div style="font-size: 14pt;">
        <div style="color: rgb(0, 0, 0); font-weight: bold;">OECD Step 3: Design and implement a strategy to respond to identified risks</div>
      </div>
      <div>
        <div style="text-align: justify; color: rgb(0, 0, 0); font-size: 11pt;"> <br>
        </div>
        <div style="text-align: justify; color: rgb(0, 0, 0); font-size: 11pt;">We implement our due diligence program and conduct supply chain analysis by leveraging information gained from independent research, engaging with civil society groups and
          rights-holders, analyzing independent third-party audits and information from upstream traceability and due diligence programs, and working directly with smelters and refiners to respond to risks identified in our supply chain.</div>
      </div>
      <br>
      <hr noshade="noshade" align="left" style="background-color: #000000; border-bottom: 0px none; border-left: 0px none; border-right: 0px none; border-top: 0px none; margin: 0px auto 0px 0px; height: 1px; width: 25%; color: #000000;">
      <div>
        <div style="color: #000000;"><sup style="vertical-align: text-top; line-height: 1; font-size: smaller;">3</sup> Available at apple.com/environment/pdf/Apple_Material_Impact_Profiles_April2019.pdf</div>
        <div style="color: #000000;"> <br>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div style="clear: both; margin-top: 10pt; margin-bottom: 10pt;" class="BRPFPageBreakArea">
        <div style="width: 100%;" class="BRPFPageFooter"><font style="font-size: 8pt;"></font>
          <div style="color: rgb(0, 0, 0);">
            <div>
              <div style="text-align: center; color: rgb(0, 0, 0); font-weight: normal; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-transform: none;">Apple Inc. | 2024 Conflict Minerals Report |&#160; <font class="BRPFPageNumber">6</font></div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div style="page-break-after: always;" class="BRPFPageBreak">
          <hr noshade="noshade" style="margin: 4px 0px; width: 100%; border-width: 0; height: 2px; color: #000000; background-color: #000000; clear: both;"></div>
        <div style="width: 100%;" class="BRPFPageHeader"></div>
      </div>
      <div>
        <div style="text-align: justify; color: rgb(0, 0, 0); font-size: 11pt;">We closely monitor completion of independent third-party audits and corrective action plans by the smelters and refiners in our supply chain. If smelters or refiners delay
          implementation of corrective action plans developed by third-party audits, we conduct applicable smelter or refiner outreach to reiterate that completion and closure of corrective action plans are required in order to remain in our supply chain.</div>
        <div><br>
        </div>
        <div style="text-align: justify; color: rgb(0, 0, 0); font-size: 11pt;">If smelters or refiners are unable or unwilling to meet our standards, we take necessary actions, through our suppliers, to terminate the applicable business relationships.
          Since 2009, Apple has directed the removal of 203 3TG smelters and refiners from our supply chain.<sup style="vertical-align: text-top; line-height: 1; font-size: smaller;">4</sup> As of December 31, 2024, we found that all identified smelters and refiners in our supply chain participated in an independent
          third-party audit that met Apple&#8217;s requirements for the responsible sourcing of minerals.</div>
        <div><br>
        </div>
        <div style="text-align: justify; color: rgb(0, 0, 0); font-size: 11pt;">Apple also works to address remaining challenges in the global supply chain for gold through its due diligence program, which is aligned with the OECD Due Diligence Guidance
          Supplement on Gold. Apple requires certification from the RMI&#8217;s Responsible Minerals Assurance Process (&#8220;RMAP&#8221;) or the London Bullion Market Association&#8217;s&#160; (&#8220;LBMA&#8221;) Responsible Gold Program for gold refiners in our supply chain. We also
          prioritize gold in our efforts to transition to recycled and renewable materials in our products, and 100 percent of our recycled gold refiners are audited.</div>
        <div><br>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div style="font-size: 14pt;">
        <div style="color: rgb(0, 0, 0); font-weight: bold;">OECD Step 4: Carry out independent third-party audits of smelter/refiner&#8217;s due diligence practices</div>
      </div>
      <div>
        <div><br>
        </div>
        <div style="text-align: justify; color: rgb(0, 0, 0); font-size: 11pt;">Since 2015, we have continued to reach a 100 percent rate of participation in independent third-party audit programs by identified 3TG smelters and refiners in our supply
          chain.</div>
        <div><br>
        </div>
        <div style="text-align: justify; color: rgb(0, 0, 0); font-size: 11pt;">Apple believes independent third-party audits remain foundational to robust due diligence systems. In particular, third-party audits play a significant role in providing
          assurance that smelters and refiners have appropriate due diligence systems in place, while helping to confirm that operations and sourcing practices are aligned with the OECD Due Diligence Guidance and do not support conflict, including in the
          DRC or adjoining countries.</div>
      </div>
      <br>
      <hr noshade="noshade" align="left" style="background-color: #000000; border-bottom: 0px none; border-left: 0px none; border-right: 0px none; border-top: 0px none; margin: 0px auto 0px 0px; height: 1px; width: 25%; color: #000000;">
      <div>
        <div style="color: rgb(0, 0, 0);"><sup style="vertical-align: text-top; line-height: 1; font-size: smaller;">4</sup> The total number of smelters and refiners directed to be removed from Apple&#8217;s supply chain since 2009 represents a cumulative count with smelters and refiners only counted once, when first removed from
          Apple&#8217;s supply chain. Smelters and refiners may subsequently re-enter the supply chain if they meet Apple&#8217;s Code and Responsible Sourcing Standard and other 3TG mineral requirements.</div>
      </div>
      <div><br>
      </div>
      <div style="clear: both; margin-top: 10pt; margin-bottom: 10pt;" class="BRPFPageBreakArea">
        <div style="width: 100%;" class="BRPFPageFooter"><font style="font-size: 8pt;"></font>
          <div style="color: rgb(0, 0, 0);">

            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div style="page-break-after: always;" class="BRPFPageBreak">
          <hr noshade="noshade" style="margin: 4px 0px; width: 100%; border-width: 0; height: 2px; color: #000000; background-color: #000000; clear: both;"></div>
        <div style="width: 100%;" class="BRPFPageHeader"></div>
      </div>
      <div>
        <div>
          <div style="font-size: 14pt;">
            <div style="color: rgb(0, 0, 0); font-weight: bold;">OECD Step 5: Report annually on supply chain due diligence</div>
          </div>
          <div>
            <div><br>
            </div>
            <div style="text-align: justify; color: rgb(0, 0, 0); font-size: 11pt;">Apple reports annually on its due diligence requirements through its Conflict Minerals Report filed with the U.S. Securities and Exchange Commission. Apple also publishes a
              list of all identified 3TG, cobalt, and lithium smelters and refiners in its supply chain, 100 percent of which participated in independent third-party audits as of December 31, 2024. In addition, we publish the following:<sup style="vertical-align: text-top; line-height: 1; font-size: smaller;">5</sup></div>

        <div><br>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div>
        <div style="color: rgb(0, 0, 0); font-size: 14pt; font-weight: bold;">Determination</div>
        <div><br>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div>
        <div style="text-align: justify; color: rgb(0, 0, 0); font-size: 11pt;">Through our smelter and refiner identification and validation process, we have identified a total of 234 smelters and refiners that processed 3TG in our supply chain during
          2024. Of all 234 smelters and refiners of 3TG determined to be in our supply chain as of December 31, 2024, Apple found no reasonable basis for concluding that any such smelter or refiner sourced 3TG that directly or indirectly financed or
          benefited armed groups in the DRC or an adjoining country. The challenges of tracking specific mineral quantities through the supply chain continue to impede the traceability of any specific mineral shipment through the entire product
          manufacturing process.</div>
        <div><br>
        </div>
        <div style="text-align: justify; color: rgb(0, 0, 0); font-size: 11pt;">Apple believes that the 3TG contained in our products originated from the countries listed in Annex I, as well as from recycled and scrap sources. Apple&#8217;s reasonable country of
          origin inquiry is based on information provided by third-party audit information received through the RMI RMAP and the LBMA, and additional information collected by Apple using other sources such as the U.S. Geological Survey, a survey of
          smelters and refiners, and third-party reviews of publicly available information. As smelters and refiners report their country of origin information at an aggregate level for all 3TG material processed by them on behalf of all of their
          customers, and not specifically for 3TG material used or processed for Apple&#8217;s supply chain, Apple cannot always determine the countries of origin of the 3TG actually contained in our specific parts and products.</div>
        <div><br>
        </div>
        <div style="text-align: justify; color: rgb(0, 0, 0); font-size: 14pt; font-weight: bold;">About this report</div>
        <div><br>
        </div>
        <div style="text-align: justify; color: rgb(0, 0, 0); font-size: 11pt;">This report has been prepared pursuant to Rule 13p-1 under the Securities Exchange Act of 1934, as amended, for the reporting period from January 1, 2024 to December 31, 2024.
          Information contained on the websites referenced in this report is not part of, or incorporated by reference into, this filing. Information presented in this report is based on calendar years.</div>
        <div><br>
        </div>
        <div style="text-align: justify; color: rgb(0, 0, 0); font-size: 11pt;">Apple believes it constitutes a &#8220;downstream&#8221; company in that Apple&#8217;s suppliers purchase cassiterite, columbite-tantalite (&#8220;coltan&#8221;), wolframite, gold, or their derivatives,
          which presently are limited to tin-, tungsten-, tantalum-, and gold-related materials after processing by smelters or refiners. Apple does not source primary material directly from mine sites.</div>
        <div><br>
        </div>
        <div style="text-align: justify; color: rgb(0, 0, 0); font-size: 11pt;">This report relates to the process undertaken in accordance with OECD Due Diligence Guidance for Apple products that were manufactured, or contracted to be manufactured, during
          2024 and that contain 3TG. These product categories are iPhone<sup style="vertical-align: text-top; line-height: 1; font-size: smaller;">&#174;</sup>, Mac<sup style="vertical-align: text-top; line-height: 1; font-size: smaller;">&#174;</sup>, iPad<sup style="vertical-align: text-top; line-height: 1; font-size: smaller;">&#174;</sup>, AirPods<sup style="vertical-align: text-top; line-height: 1; font-size: smaller;">&#174;</sup>, Apple TV<sup style="vertical-align: text-top; line-height: 1; font-size: smaller;">&#174;</sup>, Apple Watch<sup style="vertical-align: text-top; line-height: 1; font-size: smaller;">&#174;</sup>, Apple Vision Pro<sup style="vertical-align: text-top; line-height: 1; font-size: smaller;">TM</sup>, Beats<sup style="vertical-align: text-top; line-height: 1; font-size: smaller;">&#174;</sup> products, HomePod<sup style="vertical-align: text-top; line-height: 1; font-size: smaller;">&#174;</sup>,
          HomePod mini<sup style="vertical-align: text-top; line-height: 1; font-size: smaller;">&#174;</sup>, Apple Card<sup style="vertical-align: text-top; line-height: 1; font-size: smaller;">&#174;</sup>, and all Apple<sup style="vertical-align: text-top; line-height: 1; font-size: smaller;">&#174;</sup> accessories. Third-party products that Apple retails but does not manufacture or contract to manufacture are outside the scope of this report.</div>
        <div>

        <div style="width: 100%;" class="BRPFPageHeader"></div>
      </div>
      <div>
        <div style="text-align: justify; color: rgb(0, 0, 0); font-size: 11pt;">The smelters and refiners identified in this report include those producing inputs for service or spare parts contracted for manufacturing in 2024 for use in connection with
          the subsequent service of previously sold products, including products serviced in subsequent years using those parts. This report does not include smelters of tin, tungsten, or tantalum or refiners of gold where such 3TG are included in
          end-of-life service parts for products that Apple no longer manufactures or contracts to manufacture.</div>
        <div><br>
        </div>
        <div style="text-align: justify; color: rgb(0, 0, 0); font-size: 11pt;">This report&#8217;s use of the terms &#8220;smelters&#8221; and &#8220;refiners&#8221; refers to the facilities processing primary 3TG to retail purity. Apple suppliers have in some cases reported smelters
          and refiners that Apple believes are not operational or may have been misidentified as smelters and refiners. As a result, Apple continues to work with the RMI to determine accurate operational status of the smelters and refiners and to work with
          suppliers throughout its supply chain to revalidate, improve, and refine their reported information, taking into account supply chain fluctuations and other changes in status or scope and relationships over time. &#8220;Identified&#8221; smelters and
          refiners are those that (i) have been reported in a supplier&#8217;s CMRT, (ii) Apple believes are currently operational, were operational at some point during the applicable year, or, while inoperative, capable of re-engagement with minimal delay or
          effort, and (iii) otherwise meet the definition of a smelter or refiner, provided that Apple may determine to treat a third party as an identified smelter or refiner notwithstanding a reclassification of such third party or a change in its
          status. As part of its reasonable country of origin inquiry, Apple has determined that certain suppliers are utilizing at least some 3TG from secondary materials (i.e., scrap or recycled materials). Facilities that process only secondary
          materials (i.e., scrap or recycled materials) are excluded from the scope of this report.</div>
        <div><br>
        </div>
        <div style="text-align: justify; color: rgb(0, 0, 0); font-size: 11pt;">Participating smelters and refiners are those that have agreed to participate in or have been found compliant with independent third-party conflict minerals audit programs
          confirming their 3TG sourcing practices. Such programs may also include audits of traceability requirements, conformity with the OECD Due Diligence Guidance, management systems, and/or risk assessments. Independent third-party 3TG audit programs
          include the RMI&#8217;s RMAP and the LBMA&#8217;s Responsible Gold Program. Throughout this report, the audits by these programs are included in references to &#8220;independent third-party audit&#8221; programs.</div>
        <div><br>
        </div>
        <div style="text-align: justify; color: rgb(0, 0, 0); font-size: 11pt;">This report contains forward-looking statements, within the meaning of the Private Securities Litigation Reform Act of 1995, that involve risks and uncertainties.
          Forward-looking statements provide current expectations of future events based on certain assumptions and include statements about our plans to use recycled minerals in our products, the timing thereof, and any statement that does not directly
          relate to any historical or current fact. Forward-looking statements can be identified by words such as &#8220;future,&#8221; &#8220;anticipates,&#8221; &#8220;believes,&#8221; &#8220;estimates,&#8221; &#8220;expects,&#8221; &#8220;intends,&#8221; &#8220;plans,&#8221; &#8220;predicts,&#8221; &#8220;will,&#8221; &#8220;would,&#8221; &#8220;could,&#8221; &#8220;can,&#8221; &#8220;may,&#8221; and
          similar terms. Forward-looking statements are not guarantees of future performance and our actual results may differ materially from the results expressed or implied in the forward-looking statements. Factors that might cause such differences
          include, but are not limited to, effects of global or regional economic conditions, any failure to meet stated targets, goals, and commitments, and execute our strategies in the time frame expected or at all. More information on risks,
          uncertainties, and other potential factors that could affect our business and performance is included in our filings with the U.S. Securities and Exchange Commission, including in the &#8220;Risk Factors&#8221; and &#8220;Management&#8217;s Discussion and Analysis of
          Financial Condition and Results of Operations&#8221; sections of the company&#8217;s most recently filed periodic reports on Form 10-K and Form 10-Q and subsequent filings.</div>
        <div><br>
        </div>
